Motivation: 解决非线性薛定谔方程在时变狄利克雷边界条件下，传统高阶Yoshida分裂方法因稳定性限制无法应用于抛物型问题，以及现有方法需要数据微分的难题。

Method: 基于Strang方法的改进，将方程中的线性刚性部分采用有理类中点规则（rational-like midpoint rule）进行积分，使得边界值无需数据微分即可计算；在此基础上构造高阶Yoshida分裂格式。

Result: 改进后的Strang方法可适用于抛物型问题，在不进行数据微分的情况下实现局部三阶和全局二阶精度。

Conclusion: 提出的修正Strang方法有效克服了Yoshida分裂在抛物问题上的稳定性限制，为时变边界条件下的非线性薛定谔方程提供了一种无需数据微分的高效高阶数值积分方案。

Abstract: A technique is provided in this paper to integrate nonlinear Schrödinger equation with time-dependent Dirichlet boundary conditions with high-order Yoshida splittings which are based on Strang method. For that, a modification of Strang method is required in which the linear and stiff part of the equation is integrated with a rational-like version of midpoint rule for which the required boundary values can be calculated without resorting to any differentiation of data. Although Yoshida splitting (with real coefficients) cannot be applied to parabolic problems to obtain order higher than two because of stability, the modified Strang method is also applicable to such type of problems and local order $3$ and global order $2$ are also obtained without differentiation of data.

</details>


### [91] [High-order complete flux schemes for convection-diffusion equations on arbitrary subdivisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08422)
*Peng Yang,Wenyu Lei,Liwei Xu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于完全通量的有限体积方法，用于求解对流-扩散方程。该方法适用于任意网格和多边形空间，通过从PDE精确推导法向通量，自然分解为齐次部分（经典Scharfetter-Gummel通量）和非齐次部分（格林函数表达），无需校正或稳定化即可实现高阶格式。对于二次空间，该方法在任意控制体积网格上均能达到最优L²收敛，而传统方法需要特殊设计的网格。数值实验验证了其鲁棒性和最优精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有限体积方法在处理高阶格式（如二次空间）时，需要特殊设计的控制体积网格才能达到最优L²收敛，缺乏通用性。同时，标准方法直接从通量定义近似数值通量，精度受限。本文旨在开发一种不受网格限制、适用于任意多项式阶数且无需校正/稳定化的高阶有限体积方法。

Method: 提出完整通量有限体积法：从原始PDE精确推导出每个控制体积边/面上的法向通量解析表达式。该通量分解为齐次部分（经典Scharfetter-Gummel通量，处理对流主导项）和非齐次部分（通过格林函数表达，包含切向通量和源项贡献）。离散空间选定后，该格式与连续方程精确等价，无需额外校正或稳定化。

Result: 1) 对于分段线性空间，该格式实现对流主导区域的最优二阶精度，并在适度粗网格上保持正性。2) 对于二次空间，传统方法（Lagrange元/B样条）需特殊网格设计才能达到最优L²收敛，而本文方法在任意控制体积网格上始终达到最优L²收敛。3) 二维和三维数值实验验证了方法的鲁棒性和最优精度。

Conclusion: 完整通量有限体积法是一种通用、高阶、无需特殊网格设计且不需要校正或稳定化的数值方法，能在任意网格上为对流-扩散方程提供最优收敛精度，特别适用于高阶多项式空间和复杂网格环境。

Abstract: We develop a complete flux finite volume method for convection-diffusion equations that works on arbitrary meshes in two and three dimensions and for discrete spaces of any polynomial degree. Unlike standard finite volume discretizations, where the numerical flux is directly approximated from the flux definition, we derive the exact normal flux across each control volume edge/face from the underlying PDE. This exact flux splits naturally into a homogeneous part (the classical Scharfetter-Gummel flux) and an inhomogeneous part expressed via a Green's function that incorporates the tangential flux and the source term. The resulting formulation is exactly equivalent to the continuous equation and, once the discrete space is chosen, yields high-order schemes without any correction or stabilization.
  For piecewise linear spaces, the scheme achieves optimal second-order accuracy in convection-dominated regimes and can preserve positivity on moderately coarse meshes. For quadratic spaces, standard finite volume methods, based on the Lagrange elements or B-splines, fail to attain optimal $L^2$ convergence unless the control volume mesh is specially designed. The proposed complete flux scheme, however, always achieves optimal $L^2$ convergence independently of the control volume mesh. Numerical experiments in two and three dimensions confirm the robustness and optimal accuracy of the approach.

</details>


### [92] [An Iterative Method for Transient Finite Element Simulations of Non-Linear Eddy Current Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08432)
*Karl Hollaus,Haik Silm*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种对含非线性材料的涡流问题进行瞬态仿真的方法，采用电压驱动线圈、Biot-Savart场计算线圈磁场、基于隐式欧拉的时间步进法，并将非线性方程组拆分为有限元系统和电路方程分别用牛顿法求解，通过层叠铁芯圆柱线圈的基准问题验证了方法性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对含非线性材料的涡流问题，现有方法在瞬态仿真中处理电压驱动线圈与非线性的耦合时效率或精度不足，需要一种稳健且高效的数值求解方案。

Method: 采用磁矢量位有限元公式，线圈磁场通过Biot-Savart定律计算；时间离散使用隐式欧拉法；将非线性方程组拆分为有限元系统和电路方程两部分，分别用牛顿法求解，并辅以线搜索策略处理场方程的非线性。

Result: 通过一个含层叠铁芯的圆柱线圈基准问题，研究了冲击电流和层压截面平均磁通密度，并提供了详细的数值性能数据。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效处理非线性材料涡流问题的瞬态仿真，且给出了完整的基准问题细节以供结果复现与评估。

Abstract: A method is presented to carry out a transient simulation of eddy current problems with nonlinear materials. Coils are voltage-driven. The magnetic field due to currents in coils are considered by their Biot-Savart-fields. The magnetic vector potential is used in the finite element formulation. The time stepping method is based on implicit Euler. The arising nonlinear equation system is split into two parts, the common finite element system and a circuit equation. Each part is solved separately by Newton's method. Additionally, a line search is used to solve the nonlinear field equations. Inrush currents and average magnetic flux densities through cross sections of laminates of a nonlinear benchmark problem consisting of a laminated iron core inserted in a cylindrical coil are studied. All details of the numerical benchmark are given to evaluate the presented results. Numerical data describing the performance of the presented method are provided.

</details>


### [93] [Neural and Spectral Operator Surrogates on Gaussian Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08492)
*Carlo Marcati,Mario Marić,Christoph Schwab,Jakob Zech*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文证明了从可分希尔伯特空间到可分希尔伯特空间的全纯映射的有限参数谱代理和神经代理的表达率界，使用Karhunen-Loéve编码器和框架解码器的编码器-近似器-解码器架构，在输入空间高斯测度协方差特征值代数衰减假设下，获得了均方误差和一阶高斯Sobolev空间的收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为全纯映射的有限参数谱代理和神经代理建立严格的表达率理论保证，特别是在高维或无限维输入空间中，同时考虑梯度近似的误差。

Method: 采用编码器-近似器-解码器架构，Karhunen-Loéve作为编码器，框架作为解码器。两类代理：i) 谱代理：Wiener多项式混沌展开的N项截断；ii) 神经代理：使用ReLU和RePU激活函数、权重一致有界的深度前馈神经网络进行逼近。假设输入空间高斯测度协方差特征值代数衰减。

Result: 获得了均方误差的收敛速度，以及一阶高斯Sobolev空间的收敛速度（用于近似梯度的误差控制）。

Conclusion: 在协方差特征值代数衰减的假设下，谱代理和神经代理对全纯映射均具有可证明的表达率界，且神经代理能同时控制梯度近似误差。

Abstract: We prove expression rate bounds of finite-parametric, spectral and neural surrogates for holomorphic maps between separable Hilbert spaces. The surrogates have an encoder-approximator-decoder architecture, with Karhunen-Loéve encoders and frame decoders. We prove expression rate bounds for two classes of finite-parametric surrogates: i) spectral surrogates obtained by N-term truncations of Wiener polynomial chaos expansions and ii) neural surrogates obtained by approximation of parametric maps with deep feedforward neural networks, ReLU and RePU activation functions and uniformly bounded weights. We work under an algebraic decay assumption on the eigenvalues of the covariance of the Gaussian measure on the input space. We obtain convergence rates for mean-square errors, and additionally in first-order Gaussian Sobolev spaces, to account for errors in the approximation of gradients.

</details>


### [94] [Unconstrained Scheme for Geometrically Constrained Gradient Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08577)
*Sören Bartels,Lucas Bouck,Christian Palus*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于调和映射梯度流的新型时间步进方案，通过先计算无约束增量再投影到切空间，避免求解退化鞍点系统，仅需解耦的正定对称系统，显著提升计算效率，并引入后验准则和变步长策略保证稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有梯度流时间步进方案通过线性化单位长度约束求解，每步需要求解难以预条件化的退化鞍点系统，计算效率低下。

Method: 先计算无约束增量，再将增量逐点投影到约束的切空间；辅以额外稳定化项；结合可计算的后验判据与变步长过程保证稳定性。

Result: 新方案在温和步长限制下能量稳定，对单位长度约束违背具有鲁棒控制，每步仅需解耦的对称正定系统，计算效率大幅提升。

Conclusion: 数值算例验证了方案的有效性，并展示了在非线性板弯曲问题中的推广应用。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the approximation of gradient flows of harmonic maps, which serve as model problems for applications in micromagnetics, liquid crystals, and nonlinear plate bending. Harmonic maps are vector fields that are critical points of the Dirichlet energy subject to the constraint that the vector field be unit length pointwise. Most existing time-stepping schemes for gradient flows deal with the constraint by linearizing the unit length constraint at every step, which involves solving for the solution increment in the tangent space of the constraint. These schemes lead to robust control over the violation of the constraint, but require solving degenerate saddle point systems at every step that may be difficult to precondition. In this paper, we propose a scheme that first computes the unconstrained increment and then projects this increment pointwise onto the tangent space. With an additional stabilization, this scheme is energy stable under mild step size restrictions and provides robust control of the unit length constraint violation. Our new scheme only requires the solution of decoupled symmetric positive definite systems at every step, which translates to a large increase in computational efficiency. We also propose a computable a posteriori criterion and a variable time-stepping procedure that guarantee the stability of the scheme. We conclude with computational examples demonstrating the efficacy of the scheme, and present a computational extension of the scheme to nonlinear plate bending.

</details>


### [95] [Some properties of high-order nonstandard multistep multistage methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08694)
*Balint M. Takacs*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文介绍了非标准版本的多步多阶段方法，证明了其收敛性，定义了非标准一般线性方法，并展示了这些方法在保持定性性质（如有界性）的同时能达到与标准方法相同的阶数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数值分析中，标准的多步多阶段方法虽然具有较高的精度，但在大步长下可能无法保持解的有界性等定性性质。为此，作者引入非标准方法，旨在在保持计算精度的同时，确保数值解在所有正步长下都能保持重要的定性特征。

Method: 作者提出了非标准版本的多步多阶段方法，并在此基础上定义了非标准一般线性方法。通过理论分析证明了这些方法的收敛性，并验证了它们能够在所有正步长下保持有界性等定性性质。

Result: 理论证明表明，非标准方法可以达到与标准方法相同的收敛阶数，同时能够在所有正步长下保持有界性等定性性质。数值实验也验证了这些理论结果。

Conclusion: 该工作成功展示了非标准多步多阶段方法在保持计算精度的同时，能够更好地保持数值解的定性性质，为数值常微分方程求解提供了新的有效工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce nonstandard versions of multistep multistage methods. While proving the convergence of these schemes, we also define nonstandard general linear methods. We show that the nonstandard methods can attain the same order as their standard counterparts while preserving certain qualitative properties (e.g., boundedness) for all positive step sizes. These results are also demonstrated by some numerical experiments.

</details>

H. Yin,K. Yoshihara,C. Z. Yuan,J. Yuan,L. Yuan,Y. Yusa,L. Zani,F. Zeng,M. Zeyrek,B. Zhang,X. Zhao,V. Zhilich,J. S. Zhou,Q. D. Zhou,X. Y. Zhou,L. Zhu,R. Žlebčík*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文使用Belle II探测器分析408 fb⁻¹数据，通过e⁺e⁻→γa, a→γγ过程在0.17–9.80 GeV/c²质量范围内搜寻类轴子粒子(ALP)，未发现显著信号，设定了最严格的耦合上限（低至10⁻⁴ GeV⁻¹），在0.17–5.00 GeV/c²范围内为当前最强限制，较此前结果提升最多9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类轴子粒子(ALP)是标准模型诸多扩展理论预言的粒子，为可见与隐藏扇区之间提供了通过光子耦合的动机明确的门户，实验搜寻ALP对检验超出标准模型的新物理具有重要意义。

Method: 利用Belle II探测器记录的408 fb⁻¹ e⁺e⁻对撞数据，通过e⁺e⁻→γa, a→γγ过程重建末态三光子事件，在双光子不变质量谱中寻找窄峰信号，扫描质量范围0.17–9.80 GeV/c²。

Result: 未观测到显著的超出背景信号，在95%置信水平上设定了ALP产生截面和耦合常数g_{aγγ}的上限，灵敏度达10⁻⁴ GeV⁻¹量级；在0.17–5.00 GeV/c²质量范围内给出了迄今最严格的限制，较此前最优结果提升最多9倍。

Conclusion: Belle II实验在ALP-光子耦合参数空间中划定了新的排除区域，显著压缩了ALP存在的可能空间，为未来更灵敏的ALP搜寻提供了基准限制。

Abstract: Axion-like particles (ALPs) are predicted in many extensions of the Standard Model and provide a well-motivated portal between visible and hidden sectors through their coupling to photons. We search for ALPs produced in the process $e^{+}e^{-}\toγa$, $a\toγγ$, using a data sample corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $408~\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$ recorded by the Belle~II detector at the SuperKEKB $e^{+}e^{-}$ collider. Events containing three photons are used to reconstruct the ALP as a narrow peak in the di-photon invariant mass spectrum over the range $0.17 < m_{a} < 9.80~\mathrm{GeV}/c^{2}$. No significant excess above background is observed. We set 95\% confidence level upper limits on the production cross section and on the ALP-photon coupling $g_{aγγ}$, reaching sensitivities at the level of $10^{-4}~\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$. The limits are the most restrictive to date over nearly the entire mass range $0.17 < m_{a} < 5.00~\mathrm{GeV}/c^{2}$, and improve upon previous results by up to a factor 9.

</details>


### [97] [Early Career Issues in Particle Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08131)
*Saptaparna Bhattacharya*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文从早期职业研究者（ECRs）的视角，探讨了粒子物理学在Higgs玻色子发现后所面临的关键问题，包括未来对撞机布局、就业市场、科学 outreach、人工智能的挑战与机遇，以及ECR的支持网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粒子物理学在标准模型完成后面临一系列基本问题（如层级问题、暗物质、中微子质量），需要新一代实验；下一代大型装置决策将影响领域至2060年，直接冲击ECR的职业发展，因此需要系统梳理ECR面临的核心问题。

Method: 基于专题讲座内容，首先从ECR视角调研未来对撞机格局，然后通过大规模问卷调查收集ECR的主要关切和可行性建议，接着分析学术就业市场、科学 outreach 的作用以及人工智能的双刃剑效应，最后总结ECR支持网络。

Result: 揭示了ECR对未来实验方向的不确定性、就业市场竞争激烈、科学 outreach 贡献未被充分认可、AI既是工具也是挑战等关键发现；同时指出存在多种支持网络帮助ECR应对职业不确定性。

Conclusion: 未来几年的大科学装置决策将深刻影响粒子物理学长达数十年的发展，ECR作为执行者承受着直接冲击；需要从培养体系、职业支持、评价机制等方面为ECR创造更可持续的发展环境。

Abstract: Particle physics stands at a crossroads. The discovery of the Higgs boson in 2012 completed the Standard Model, but it also opened a new set of fundamental questions about the nature of our universe. Answering these questions, from the hierarchy problem and the nature of dark matter to the origin of neutrino masses, will require a new generation of powerful and sophisticated experiments. The global community is currently engaged in a comprehensive, long-term planning process to determine the next big machine. These discussions are not merely technical. The decisions made in the coming years will shape the field until 2060 and beyond. This extended timeline has a direct and immediate impact on the lives and careers of the field's youngest members: the graduate students, postdoctoral fellows, and other early-career researchers (ECRs) who will be tasked with designing, building, and ultimately analyzing the data from these future facilities. This proceeding provides an overview of the key issues facing ECRs in particle physics today, based on a talk delivered on the subject. It begins by surveying the future collider landscape from an ECR perspective. It then delves into the critical feedback gathered directly from ECRs through large-scale surveys, focusing on their primary concerns and actionable recommendations. Following this, it presents an analysis of the academic job market, the important but often unrewarded role of science outreach, and the emerging dual-edged challenge and opportunity of artificial intelligence. The paper concludes by highlighting the indispensable support networks available to ECRs, which are vital for navigating this complex and uncertain career path.

</details>

### [98] [On the Self-adjoint properties of the standard Whittaker $(\mathfrak{g}, K)$-modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08245)
*Kenji Taniguchi*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了实分裂约化线性李群情形下的标准Whittaker (g, K)-模结构，证明了其自伴性，并给出了秩2分裂群SL(3,R)、Sp(2,R)和G2(分裂)的显式socle滤过。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解标准Whittaker模在实分裂约化李群中的结构性质，特别是其在Harish-Chandra (g, K)-模范畴中作为内射对象的作用及全局特征。

Method: 利用Harish-Chandra模范畴理论，分析标准Whittaker模的内射性和全局特征，通过自伴性证明和显式socle滤过计算进行研究。

Result: 证明了标准Whittaker (g, K)-模具有自伴结构，确定了其全局特征，并给出了秩2分裂群SL(3,R)、Sp(2,R)和G2(分裂)的显式socle滤过。

Conclusion: 标准Whittaker模在实分裂约化李群中具有自伴性和内射性，其结构可通过socle滤过显式描述，为理解更高秩情形提供了基础。

Abstract: The structure of the standard Whittaker $(\mathfrak{g}, K)$-module is examined in the case when the group in question is a real split reductive linear Lie group. This module is an injective object in the category of Harish-Chandra $(\mathfrak{g}, K)$-modules which admit a fixed infinitesimal character. The global character of this module is determined. The main theorem of this paper is that it has a self-adjoint structure. Also obtained are the explicit socle filtrations of the standard Whittaker $(\mathfrak{g}, K)$-modules for the rank two split groups $SL(3,\mathbb{R})$, $Sp(2,\mathbb{R})$ and $G_{2}$(split).

</details>


### [99] [Representations of $p$-adic reductive groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08308)
*David Renard*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本书系统介绍了p-adic约化群的（复）表示理论，从基础概念出发，逐步深入到Bernstein中心和Langlands分类定理，共七章及附录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为研究生及以上水平读者提供关于p-adic约化群光滑表示理论的完整叙述，特别是Bernstein分解定理和Langlands分类定理这两大核心成果。

Method: 前四章建立一般性框架：含幂等元的代数、全不连通局部紧空间与群、光滑表示及其特殊类（紧、酉、平方可积）；第五章回顾p-adic约化群的结构；第六章研究光滑表示范畴（Bernstein分解与中心描述）；第七章处理平方可积与温和表示并证明Langlands分类定理。

Result: 建立了Bernstein分解定理，描述了光滑表示范畴的中心，并证明了Langlands分类定理，完整刻画了不可约光滑表示。

Conclusion: 本书为p-adic约化群表示理论提供了从基础到前沿的系统性论述，核心贡献在于对Bernstein中心和Langlands分类的严格证明与阐释。

Abstract: This book presents a part of the (complex) representation theory of $p$-adic reductive groups. Starting from a basis accessible to graduate students, it culminates with the theory of the "Bernstein center" and the Langlands classification of irreducible smooth representations.
  \vspace{0.5cm}
  \noindent The book consists of seven chapters, Chapters VI and VII constituting the core of the book. Chapter VI is devoted to the study of the category of smooth representations of a $p$-adic reductive group, establishing among other things the Bernstein decomposition theorem and the description of its center. Chapter VII deals with square-integrable and tempered representations, and the Langlands classification theorem is proved there.
  \vspace{0.5cm}
  \noindent The first four chapters are placed in a more general framework and tackle, in order, the study of algebras with idempotents, totally disconnected locally compact spaces and groups, smooth representations of the latter, and particular classes of representations (compact, unitary, square-integrable). Chapter V is a review of the structure results of $p$-adic reductive groups. Appendices give the elements of category theory and some results in algebra used in the text.

</details>


### [100] [The fundamental theorems of invariant theory for linearly oligomorphic groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08485)
*Alessandro Danelon,Andrew Snowden*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文为Harman等人引入的推广经典群的新型无穷维代数群建立了不变性理论的第一基本定理和第二基本定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Harman等人最近引入的推广经典群的无穷维代数群建立对应的不变性理论基础。

Method: 基于Harman和另一位作者先前引入的无穷维代数群框架，推导并证明其不变性理论的第一和第二基本定理。

Result: 成功建立了这些新型无穷维代数群的不变性理论第一和第二基本定理版本。

Conclusion: 将经典不变性理论推广到了新引入的无穷维代数群上，完善了其理论基础。

Abstract: In recent work, Harman and the second author introduced some new infinite dimensional algebraic groups that generalize the classical groups. In this paper, we establish versions of the first and second fundamental theorems of invariant theory for them.

</details>

### [101] [College closures from 2020 to 2025: An exploratory analysis and its implications for the enrollment cliff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07917)
*Sepehr Akbari,Shahrzad Jamshidi*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本研究收集了2020-2025年间65所关闭或合并的美国高校数据，综合运用贝叶斯模型、降维、聚类和主题建模方法，分析了这些院校的地理分布、共同特征及媒体报道模式，发现关闭集中在东北部和中西部，关闭院校在财务结构、区域人口统计和办学使命上呈现异质性，宗教关联在媒体报道中尤为突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新冠疫情引发的高校关闭与合并浪潮，可能为未来十年预期的"入学悬崖"（demographic enrollment cliff）提供一个早期但不完美的预演，作者希望通过分析已发生的关闭事件，为即将面临人口结构冲击的高等教育机构提供参考。

Method: 构建包含65所关闭/合并院校的数据集，配以院校特征、州级和区域级人口/经济/金融指标，并补充新闻报道语料库；采用贝叶斯模型、降维分析、聚类分析和主题建模相结合的方法进行多维度分析。

Result: 关闭事件在东北部和中西部更为频繁（与人口预测一致），但绝对数量仍然较小；关闭院校呈现异质性而非统一模式，财务结构、区域人口特征和办学使命是区分不同关闭类型的关键因素，宗教关联在媒体报道中反复出现。

Conclusion: 鉴于样本量较小，作者将结果定位为探索性和描述性分析，并讨论了这些发现对面临入学悬崖的院校可能意味着什么、以及不能意味着什么。

Abstract: The COVID-19 pandemic produced a modest wave of college closures and mergers that may offer an early, if imperfect, preview of the demographic "enrollment cliff" anticipated in the coming decade. This paper examines the institutions that closed or merged between 2020 and the end of 2025. We assemble a dataset of 65 such institutions, pairing institutional characteristics with state- and regional-level demographic, economic, and financial indicators, and supplement it with a corpus of news coverage of the closures. Using a combination of Bayesian models, dimensionality reduction, clustering, and topic modeling, we describe where these closures occurred, what the closed institutions had in common, and how they were discussed publicly. Consistent with prior demographic projections, closures were more frequent in the Northeast and Midwest, though the absolute numbers remain small. The closed institutions were heterogeneous rather than uniform: financial structure, regional demographics, and institutional mission each contributed to distinguishing them, and religious affiliation recurred prominently in media coverage. We frame these results as exploratory and descriptive given the small sample, and we discuss what they may, and may not, imply for institutions navigating the enrollment cliff.

</details>


### [102] [Causal Effects of Protocol-Fee Changes on Liquidity Provision in Automated Market Makers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08525)
*Wen-Ting Wang*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文利用Uniswap协议费用开关带来的LP费率变化，通过匹配重叠事件研究双重差分设计，估计了流动性提供者对费率削减的供给响应（K_L），发现短期内活跃流动性和局部深度无显著变化，LP参与和组成也无明显反应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AMM费用规则评估常以LP福利为目标，但该目标混合了费用收入、逆向选择损失（LVR）、路由响应和流动性供给等多个渠道。固定费用的Uniswap v3历史数据无法分离这些渠道，也无法评估反事实的动态费用规则。作者利用Uniswap协议费用开关带来的真实费率变化来识别LP侧的供给响应。

Method: 采用预先指定的匹配重叠事件研究双重差分设计（matched-overlap event-study difference-in-differences），利用Uniswap协议费用开关（protocol-fee switch）对LP费率的差异化削减作为外生变化。从公开日志中重构处理、事件时间、单元角色和结果，构建冻结的哈希校验面板数据集，估计流动性供给对费率削减的响应K_L。

Result: 短期内未检测到活跃流动性或局部深度的大幅平均响应；LP参与度和构成（估计更精确）同样无显著变化。结果是在设计分辨率下的非检测，而非精确零。Token-1交易量和原生费用收入未通过平行趋势检验，仅作描述性报告。

Conclusion: 通过渠道可容忍性审计，LP侧响应K_L是基于设计识别的估计量，而面向交易者的动态费用保护是模型条件边界，而非第二个估计量。说明当前设计能识别的仅是LP侧费率传导效应，无法同时识别交易者侧动态费用效应。

Abstract: Automated market maker (AMM) fee rules are often evaluated by liquidity-provider (LP) welfare, but that objective mixes fee revenue, adverse-selection loss (loss-versus-rebalancing, LVR), routing response, and liquidity supply. Fixed-fee Uniswap v3 history cannot separate these channels or identify counterfactual trader-facing dynamic-fee rules. Real fee-related variation nonetheless exists: the Uniswap protocol-fee switch cut LP take-rates with tier-differentiated intensity while leaving trader-facing fees unchanged. Using a pre-specified matched-overlap event-study difference-in-differences design, we estimate the liquidity-supply response to take-rate cuts, the kernel K_L that simulator-based fee-controller evaluations routinely freeze, while reconstructing treatment, event time, unit roles, and outcomes from public logs into a frozen, hash-checked panel before any estimate. We detect no large short-run average response in active liquidity or local depth; LP participation and composition, more precisely estimated, likewise show none, so the result is a non-detection at the design's resolution rather than a precise zero. Token-1 volume and native fee income fail the parallel-trends gate and are reported descriptively. A channel-admissibility audit delimits the estimand: the LP-side response K_L is design-based, while trader-facing dynamic-fee protection is a model-conditioned boundary, not a second estimand.

</details>


### [103] [Accounting for overdispersion and clustering in binomial data from N-of-1 trials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08722)
*Majnu John,Heejung Bang,Stephanie Winkelbeiner,Philipp Homan*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种针对N-of-1试验（以二项变量为主要结局）的汇总分析方法：第一种考虑超二项变异，第二种同时考虑层次聚类和过度离散，并通过真实数据分析和模拟比较验证了方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: N-of-1试验虽以个体患者结果为主要目标，但汇总跨患者结果同样具有临床和研究意义，因此需要合适的统计分析方法来处理二项结局变量的汇总问题。

Method: 提出了两种分析策略：方法一考虑超二项变异（extra-binomial variation），方法二在过度离散基础上进一步纳入层次聚类（hierarchical clustering）结构。

Result: 通过真实数据分析和模拟研究对两种方法进行了说明和比较。

Conclusion: 两种方法为N-of-1试验中二项结局变量的跨患者汇总提供了可行的分析路径，其中第二种方法能更全面地处理数据中的聚类结构和离散特征。

Abstract: N-of-1 trials are patient centered randomized controlled trials. Although the primary goal of N-of-1 trials is to obtain the results for each patient separately, pooling the results across patients also has relevance. In this paper, we present two analytical strategies to pool the results across N-of-1 trials, when the main outcome for each patient is a binomial variable. Our first method takes into account the extra-binomial variation, while as the second approach takes into account hierarchical clustering in addition to overdispersion. We illustrate the methods using real data analysis and compare the methods using simulations.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>

# cond-mat.str-el [[Back]](#toc)

### [104] [Anisotropic vacancy-induced magnetization textures in altermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07789)
*Ruben Burkard,Mathias S. Scheurer,Urban F. P. Seifert*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过微观模拟和低能场论，研究了交变磁体中由空位引起的磁纹理，发现空位会普遍产生实空间各向异性的磁序畸变，其结构编码了底层交变磁态的对称性，这种杂质响应为局域分辨探针检测交变磁序提供了直接途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索空位缺陷在交变磁体中诱导的磁纹理，并利用这种杂质响应作为检测交变磁序的手段。

Method: 采用微观模拟和低能场论方法，分别研究了经典交变磁体（横向磁场中空位产生各向异性磁化纹理）和量子模型（零场下涨落诱导纵向幂律衰减磁畸变）两种情况。

Result: 空位在交变磁体中会产生实空间各向异性的磁序畸变，其结构反映了底层交变磁态的对称性。经典情形下在横向磁场中产生各向异性磁化纹理；量子模型下在零场中涨落诱导纵向幂律衰减磁畸变。

Conclusion: 空位诱导的磁纹理为利用局域分辨探针直接检测交变磁序提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: We study magnetic textures induced by vacancies in altermagnets using microscopic simulations and low-energy field theory. We show that a vacancy generically produces a real-space anisotropic distortion of the magnetic order, whose structure encodes the symmetry of the underlying altermagnetic state. This impurity response offers a direct route to detecting altermagnetic order with locally resolved probes. We demonstrate this for both classical altermagnets, where vacancies generate anisotropic magnetization textures in a transverse magnetic field, and quantum models, where fluctuations induce longitudinal power-law decaying magnetic distortions even at zero field.

</details>


### [105] [Electron-Phonon Functional Renormalization Group of Fermi Liquid Instabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07804)
*C. Alexander Baum,Matteo Dürrnagel,Ronny Thomale,Lennart Klebl*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文构建了关联电子模型的功能性重整化群（FRG）方案，该方案同时包含电子相互作用和来自色散声子带的电子-声子耦合（EPC）。通过对电子-电子相互作用顶点的RG流进行特定化，我们在相同框架下处理了声子介导和电子介导的费米液体不稳定性，分析了电荷、自旋、向列性和超导配对等候选电子有序参量。以方晶格上耦合声学声子带的Hubbard模型中的Peierls型相变为示例进行了说明。该方法能够纳入完整的电子和声子第一性原理输入，从而在微观最实质层面上分析纠缠的电子相互作用与EPC导致的电子有序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在关联电子体系中，电子-电子相互作用和电子-声子耦合常常相互纠缠，共同决定电子有序相的涌现。现有方法难以在同一理论框架下平等地处理这两类相互作用诱导的费米液体不稳定性，缺乏能够同时纳入完整第一性原理电子和声子输入的微观分析方法。

Method: 提出一个扩展的功能性重整化群（FRG）方案，将色散声子带及其与电子的耦合纳入FRG框架。通过对电子-电子相互作用顶点的RG流进行分析，将声子介导和电子介导的费米液体不稳定性放在平等地位处理，统一分析电荷、自旋、向列性和超导配对等序参量的竞争。

Result: 以方晶格Hubbard模型耦合声学声子带为例，成功再现了Peierls型相变，验证了该方法处理电子相互作用与EPC交织体系的有效性。该方法具备将完整的ab initio电子和声子数据作为输入的能力。

Conclusion: 该FRG方法为在微观最实质层面上分析纠缠的电子相互作用与EPC导致的电子有序提供了统一的理论框架，可推广至实际材料的第一性原理研究。

Abstract: We formulate a functional renormalization group (FRG) ansatz for correlated electron models that incorporates electronic interactions as well as electron-phonon coupling (EPC) stemming from dispersive phonon bands. Particularizing to the RG flow of the electron-electron interaction vertex, we treat phonon- and electron-mediated Fermi liquid instabilities on equal footing as we analyze tentative electronic order parameters related to charge, spin, nematicity, and superconducting pairing. We illustrate the approach at the example of Peierls-type transitions we find for the Hubbard model on the square lattice coupled to acoustic phonon bands. Our method allows to incorporate full electronic and phononic ab initio input, and thus lends itself to the analysis of electronic order from intertwined electronic interactions and EPC at a microscopically most substantiated level.

</details>


### [106] [Perturbations in the spin-orbital liquid (the Yao-Lee model)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07869)
*A. M. Tsvelik*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了Yao-Lee模型中自旋轨道液体在多种微扰下的稳定性，发现Kitaev相互作用能使visons获得流动性，从而改变体系的精确可解性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Yao-Lee模型在近期提出的微观实现中自然出现的多种微扰下，自旋轨道液体相的稳定性，特别是哪些微扰会破坏其精确可解性。

Method: 对Yao-Lee模型引入多种自然出现的微扰，分别分析各微扰的关联性（relevance/irrelevance），并特别研究Kitaev相互作用对$\mathbb{Z}_2$规范场的影响。

Result: 大多数微扰是无关的，不会定性改变精确可解性；但Kitaev相互作用会耦合到$\mathbb{Z}_2$规范场，使visons变得可移动，从而对体系产生定性影响。

Conclusion: Yao-Lee模型的自旋轨道液体相对大多数自然微扰是稳定的，但Kitaev相互作用是一个关键例外，它通过使visons获得流动性来破坏体系的精确可解性。

Abstract: I investigate the stability of the spin-orbital liquid described by the Yao-Lee model in the presence of multiple perturbations that naturally arise in a recently proposed microscopic realization. While most perturbations are irrelevant and do not qualitatively alter the exact solvability, we find that the Kitaev interaction plays a special role: it couples to the $ \mathbb{Z}_2 $ gauge field and renders the visons mobile.

</details>


### [107] [Tunable Emergent Gauge Fields from Skyrmions in a Quasicrystalline Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07948)
*Leandro M. Chinellato,Flavia A. Gómez Albarracín,Cristian D. Batista,Pablo S. Cornaglia*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究二维准晶格中的磁性斯格明子，发现准周期钉扎势的准简并极小值使得斯格明子密度在饱和场附近可准连续变化，与周期晶格中的一级相变行为截然不同，从而实现拓扑荷和霍尔电导率的平滑调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究准周期晶格中磁性斯格明子的行为，特别是其与周期晶格中不同的相变机制，以及如何利用准周期钉扎势实现对拓扑荷和霍尔电导率的平滑调控。

Method: 使用经典海森堡模型，包含Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用和外磁场，在二维准晶格上对磁性斯格明子进行数值模拟研究。

Result: 准周期钉扎势的准简并极小值使得斯格明子密度在接近饱和场时可准连续抑制，与周期晶格中强一级崩塌形成鲜明对比；霍尔电导率可通过小幅磁场变化平滑调至零。

Conclusion: 准晶磁体可作为可调拓扑纹理的平台，通过场控多稳态机制实现拓扑荷和磁电响应的平滑调控，在磁性存储和磁电子学响应中具有潜在应用。

Abstract: We study magnetic skyrmions in a two-dimensional quasicrystalline lattice using a classical Heisenberg model with Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interactions and an external magnetic field. The competition between the skyrmion-skyrmion repulsion and an emergent quasiperiodic pinning landscape gives rise to a sequence of distinct skyrmion lattice configurations as a function of field. The resulting hierarchy of quasiperiodic pinning potentials, characterized by closely spaced quasi-degenerate minima, enables a quasi-continuous suppression of the skyrmion density as the saturation field is approached, in sharp contrast to the strongly first-order collapse of skyrmion crystals on periodic lattices. This provides a direct mechanism for controlling the topological charge and, consequently, the emergent gauge field for itinerant electrons. As a consequence, the Hall conductivity can be strongly modified with small changes in the magnetic field and driven smoothly to zero near saturation. This field-controlled tunability, rooted in the underlying multistability, identifies quasicrystalline magnets as a platform for tunable topological textures, with potential applications in magnetic memory and magnetoelectronic response.

</details>


### [108] [Scalable Simulation of Strongly Correlated Electron-Phonon Systems via Non-Gaussian Matrix Product States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08176)
*Siyuan Jiang,Tao Shi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用非高斯矩阵乘积态方法研究强关联电子-声子耦合系统，在一维和二维Hubbard-Holstein模型中揭示了相分离、金属相、条纹相等多种量子相，并阐明了局域与非局域延迟效应对电荷序和自旋涨落的调控机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联电子-声子系统中电子与声子之间的纠缠难以有效表征，尤其是在声子具有无界希尔伯特空间的情况下，传统方法难以实现可扩展模拟。本文旨在开发一种能够高效处理这种纠缠并适用于宽参数区间的数值方法。

Method: 将非高斯态与矩阵乘积态相结合，构建非高斯矩阵乘积态方法，用于模拟具有无界希尔伯特空间的声子模式与强关联电子之间的纠缠。

Result: 在一维广义Hubbard-Holstein模型中观察到显著的相分离倾向；在二维半满模型中构建了相图，发现非局域声子介导吸引与局域Hubbard排斥竞争产生了金属相；在掺杂体系中揭示了软声子稳定条纹相的作用；在反铁磁中发现局域延迟效应使电荷序被声子钉扎；在掺杂电荷密度波区发现非局域延迟效应抑制相分离并稳定双极化子条纹相。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一种系统研究电子-声子相互作用的路径，这种相互作用对超导机制至关重要。

Abstract: We investigate strongly correlated electron-phonon (e-ph) systems via a non-Gaussian matrix product state method. By combining non-Gaussian states with matrix product states, our method efficiently characterizes the intractable entanglement between strongly correlated electrons and phononic modes of unbounded Hilbert space, enabling scalable simulations across broad parameter regimes. In one-dimensional generalized Hubbard--Holstein (HH) models, we identify a pronounced tendency toward phase separation (PS), an instability relevant to recent angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy observations on doped cuprate chain. In two-dimensional HH models, we construct the phase diagram at half-filling featuring a metallic phase emerging from the competition between non-local phonon-mediated attraction and local Hubbard repulsion. Upon doping, we elucidate the role of soft phonons in stabilizing stripe phases. In the antiferromagnet, the stabilization of the fully filled stripe is attributed to a local retardation effect, wherein the charge order is pinned by phonons, leading to a diminished response to spin fluctuations. In the doped charge-density-wave regime, a novel bipolaronic stripe phase with an enlarged unit cell is stabilized via a non-local retardation effect, where long-range phonon-mediated interactions suppress PS. Our work establishes a systematic route to decoding the e-ph interplay that is crucial for superconductivity.

</details>


### [109] [Emergent Topology from Nonlocal Electronic Correlations in One Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08278)
*Félix Fossati,Erik Linnér,Evgeny A. Stepanov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文证明，在一维扩展Hubbard模型中，通过调节非局域库仑相互作用，可以从拓扑平庸的非相互作用能带结构中诱导出具有非平庸拓扑特性的键序波(BOW)和电荷密度波(CDW)相，前者映射为SSH模型，后者映射为Rice-Mele模型，均表现出局域边缘态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索低维系统中电子关联如何从拓扑平庸的非相互作用能带结构中诱导出拓扑相，揭示非局域电子相互作用产生 emergent 拓扑的机制。

Method: 采用先进的团簇图解法多体方法（cluster-diagrammatic many-body approach），对一维扩展Hubbard模型进行分析，通过调节非局域库仑相互作用驱动相变，并利用有效低能单粒子描述将BOW和CDW相分别映射为SSH模型和Rice-Mele模型。

Result: 非局域库仑相互作用驱动BOW和CDW相的出现；BOW相映射为SSH模型，CDW相（具有次主导的键序关联）映射为Rice-Mele模型；两相均具有非平庸拓扑特征，表现为局域边缘态的存在。

Conclusion: 非局域电子关联能在关联系统中产生 emergent 拓扑，这为在低维关联体系中设计和实现拓扑相提供了新的物理机制。

Abstract: We demonstrate that electronic correlations in low-dimensional systems can induce topological phases starting from a topologically trivial noninteracting band structure. Using an advanced cluster-diagrammatic many-body approach applied to the one-dimensional extended Hubbard model, we show that tuning the nonlocal Coulomb interaction drives the emergence of bond-order-wave (BOW) and charge-density-wave (CDW) phases. Despite being interaction-driven and symmetry-broken, these states admit an effective low-energy single-particle description. In particular, the BOW phase maps onto an effective Su-Schrieffer-Heeger model, while the CDW phase, with subleading bond-order correlations, corresponds to a Rice-Mele model. Both phases exhibit a nontrivial topological character, manifested by the presence of localized edge states. Our results establish a mechanism by which nonlocal electronic correlations generate emergent topology in correlated systems.

</details>


### [110] [The charge density fluctuations and the Shrinking Fermi Liquid scenario for strange metallicity in cuprates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08476)
*Sergio Caprara,Sauri Bhattacharyya,Carlo Di Castro,Giovanni Mirarchi,Götz Seibold,Marco Grilli*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文基于收缩费米液体理论，通过电荷密度涨落、声子和顺磁子等实验表征的物理成分，解释了过掺杂铜氧化物在超导转变温度以上的奇异金属输运性质，并提出了对数增长的耗散参数γ以统一解释低温输运和热力学行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释铜氧化物在略微过掺杂区域中奇异金属性质的微观物理起源，特别是在电荷密度波量子临界点隐藏于超导穹顶之下的背景下，利用实验上已表征的电荷密度涨落、声子和粒子-空穴激发等成分，构建一个自洽的理论框架。

Method: 采用收缩费米液体理论框架，将实验表征的短程、非临界电荷密度涨落（其特征能量M/γ~10 meV）与声子及顺磁子连续谱作为散射源，计算费米液体准粒子的输运和光学响应，并引入对数增长的耗散参数γ以描述低温下的行为。

Result: 成功解释了超导转变温度以上奇异金属的电阻率、光学性质（包括ω/T标度行为）、比热、塞贝克系数、热输运和磁电阻等输运与热力学性质。

Conclusion: 基于实验表征的有限寿命电荷密度涨落与声子、顺磁子的组合散射机制，可以为铜氧化物的奇异金属相提供一个自洽、统一的理论描述。

Abstract: We interpret the strange metal (SM) properties of slightly overdoped cuprates in terms of the recently proposed Shrinking Fermi Liquid theory. This is based on the pervading presence in the cuprate phase diagram of charge density fluctuations (CDF), which have been identified and characterized in RIXS. These fluctuations are abundant and have a low energy due to the proximity of the charge density wave quantum critical point hidden under the superconducting dome of cuprates, but have a short range and non-critical character with a finite energy M/γ~10 meV as measured above Tc. Here M~ ξ^-2 is determined by the short correlation length ξ, while γencodes the Landau damping ruling the lifetime of the charge fluctuations. Besides these low energy CDF, cuprates also display phonons and a broad continuum of particle-hole excitations, mostly due to spin paramagnons arising from their strongly correlated character. With these experimentally characterized ingredients we show that above Tc the SM properties in transport are well described in terms of fermionic Landau quasiparticles scattering with CDF and phonons. The optical properties can instead be interpreted by the combined effect of low energy CDF determining the temperature dependence, and of the paramagnon continuum determining a linear in frequency scattering rate. Remarkably, the combined effect of these simple ingredients also induces ω/T scaling properties for frequencies larger than M/γ. When superconductivity is suppressed by strong magnetic fields the SM properties extend down to a few Kelvin. By assuming that the CDF dissipation parameter γgrows logarithmically by lowering T, we account for all anomalous transport and thermodynamic properties of cuprates (specific heat, Seebeck, heat transport, resistivity, and magnetoresistance) thereby providing a consistent scenario for the SM phase of cuprates.

</details>


### [111] [Holographic Theory of Mixed-Dimensional Statistics and Conservation-Encoding Hopping-Operator Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08583)
*Hanyu Xue,Xiao-Gang Wen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个混合维度激发统计的通用框架，通过跳跃算子代数(LOsA)定义微观统计性质，将守恒律与激发统计联系起来。指出指向性守恒律对应的可逆激发由高阶群$cG$的对称缺陷描述，其统计性质由上同调类$[ω] ∈ H^{d+2}(BcG;R/Z)$分类；更一般的非指向性守恒律与非可逆激发则由融合$d$-范畴分类，对应广义对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的费米统计（具有守恒粒子数）推广到受交织守恒律约束的混合维度激发系统，发展一个通用的统计分类框架。

Method: 1. 定义跳跃算子代数(LOsA)——由在保持守恒律下局部移动或形变现激发的算子生成的局部算子子代数。2. 对于指向性守恒律，证明LOsA由上同调类$[ω] ∈ H^{d+2}(BcG;R/Z)$分类，并通过全息实现（边界上的高阶群规范理论）给出物理解释。3. 对于非指向性守恒律，证明LOsA及其希尔伯特空间表示等价于融合$d$-范畴的分类。

Result: 指向性守恒律的激发统计由上同调类$[ω] ∈ H^{d+2}(BcG;R/Z)$完全分类；非指向性守恒律的激发统计由融合$d$-范畴分类。高阶群结果是融合$d$-范畴分类在完全指向情形下的特例。

Conclusion: 该框架统一描述了混合维度激发统计，建立了守恒律、激发统计与广义对称性之间的对应关系，为研究拓扑相和量子物质中的激发行为提供了系统分类工具。

Abstract: We develop a general framework for the statistics of mixed-dimensional excitations subject to intertwined conservation laws, extending the familiar Fermi statistics with conserved particle number. We define statistics microscopically through a \emph{hopping-operator algebra}: a local operator subalgebra (LOsA) generated by operators that locally move or deform excitations while preserving the conservation law. Nontrivial statistics arise when this subalgebra is nontrivial.
  We first focus on LOsAs that encode \emph{pointed} conservation laws. These give rise to invertible excitations, whose fusion rules are exactly those of the symmetry defects of a higher group $\cG$. For such $\cG$-conserved excitations in $d$-dimensional space, we show that the corresponding LOsA -- and hence the statistics it defines -- is classified by a cohomology class $[ω] \in H^{d+2}(B\cG;\R/\Z)$, where changing $[ω]$ by a coboundary corresponds merely to a rephasing of the local operators. We further provide a holographic realization: excitations with this prescribed conservation law and statistics live on the boundary of a $\cG$ higher-group gauge theory in $(d+1)$-dimensional space, twisted by $[ω]$.
  More generally, non-pointed conservation laws and the associated statistics of non-invertible excitations are defined by a pair: a LOsA together with its excitation-complex representation. This is equivalent to the pair consisting of a LOsA and its Hilbert-space representation, which is the data defining a generalized symmetry. Consequently, non-pointed conservation laws and their statistics in $d$-dimensional space are classified by fusion $d$-categories, just as generalized symmetries are. The higher-group results above are the fully-pointed special cases of this more general classification.

</details>


### [112] [Accurate Self-Attention Wavefunctions at Large Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08616)
*Filippo Gaggioli,Sam Azadi,Liang Fu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文证明自注意力波函数（self-attention wavefunctions）在二维均匀电子气中可扩展至169个粒子，能量精度超越最先进的扩散蒙特卡洛（DMC）方法，并成功恢复完整集体模式色散，且N=91和N=169的观测值近乎完美一致，表明已收敛到热力学极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自注意力神经网络变分波函数虽具有超越传统变分ansatze的表达能力，但其计算复杂度也随之增加，因此核心动机是探究这类波函数在大系统尺寸下是否仍能保持准确性，即可扩展性问题。

Method: 将自注意力波函数应用于二维均匀电子气系统，粒子数最多达N=169，通过变分蒙特卡洛方法获得基态能量和波函数，进而恢复液相的全集体模式色散（从小q等离激元支到q≈2k_F附近的旋子-like极小值）。

Result: 自注意力波函数在N=169时仍能获得系统性低于最先进DMC的能量；成功恢复从等离激元到旋子-like极小值的完整集体模式色散；N=91和N=169的观测值近乎完美一致，表明已收敛到热力学极限。

Conclusion: 自注意力波函数在大系统尺寸下（至少到169个粒子）仍能保持高精度，展现出良好的可扩展性，并且能够直接获取基态波函数以计算超出能量之外的物理观测量（如集体模式色散），是研究强关联电子体系的有效工具。

Abstract: Self-attention neural networks provide powerful variational wavefunctions that surpass the expressivity of traditional variational ansatze. This expressivity, however, comes with increased computational complexity, raising a pressing question about scalability -- can such wavefunctions retain their accuracy at large system sizes? We apply self-attention wavefunctions to the two-dimensional homogeneous electron gas for up to N=169 particles, obtaining energies systematically lower than state-of-the-art DMC. Direct access to the ground state wavefunction further lets us recover the full collective-mode dispersion of the liquid phase, from the small-q plasmon branch to a roton-like minimum near q=2k_F. Observables at N=91 and N=169 are in near-perfect agreement, indicating convergence to the thermodynamic limit.

</details>
